# Third-party notices

This project's own code is licensed under Apache-2.0. Third-party code, generated bindings, and assets retain their original licenses. This file preserves applicable copyright and license notices and identifies where corresponding source can be obtained.

Include the applicable notices and source links with distributed applications or accompanying materials. Framework peers and native dependencies resolved by a host application may have different versions and additional transitive dependencies; keep the notices from that actual build as well. Build/test-only tools are not represented as bundled application code.

## Package scope

`@react-native-rich-text-editor/code-highlighting` — syntect 5.3.0, two-face 0.5.1, their dependencies, and bundled grammar/theme assets. The base editor is a peer dependency and supplies its own notices.

## Rust dependencies

This inventory is the union of the locked normal and build dependency graphs for all seven shipped iOS/Android Rust targets. Build dependencies and procedural macros are included conservatively; test-only and optional CLI-only tools are not represented as shipped runtime code. Upstream alternative license texts are retained as supplied; their presence does not convert an OR choice into an AND requirement.

The Rust standard library and compiler runtime notices are supplied separately in [RUST-STANDARD-LIBRARY-NOTICES.html](RUST-STANDARD-LIBRARY-NOTICES.html), copied from the pinned Rust 1.95.0 distribution.

## Syntect default theme notices

Syntect 5.3.0 embeds seven default themes independently of Two Face's grammar/theme collection: `base16-ocean.dark`, `base16-ocean.light`, `base16-eighties.dark`, `base16-mocha.dark`, `InspiredGitHub`, `Solarized (dark)`, and `Solarized (light)`.

## Provenance

The installed crate's `.cargo_vcs_info.json` identifies source commit `e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27`. Its `assets/default.themedump` exactly matches that upstream commit (SHA-256 `8b57a2118224993360b6fc5fc2fa2e9872a827f00f9c57d43da08fa42c892399`). [`src/dumps.rs`](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/src/dumps.rs#L207) names the seven themes and includes that dump; the [`Makefile`](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/Makefile) runs `gendata themepack testdata assets/default.themedump`.

The source tree's gitlinks and [`.gitmodules`](https://github.com/trishume/syntect/blob/e4670846ecf16d8832db6c43d531bec466214e27/.gitmodules) pin:

| Theme files | Source repository | Exact submodule commit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `InspiredGitHub.tmTheme` | sethlopezme/InspiredGitHub.tmtheme | `18ddb271179e118cfc2dd83abf88b915b7328a25` |
| `Solarized (dark).tmTheme`, `Solarized (light).tmTheme` | braver/Solarized | `bcd6234b4f5f96d3fd27db079268b5757053072a` |
| Four `base16-*.tmTheme` files | kkga/spacegray | `2703e93f559e212ef3895edd10d861a4383ce93d` |

The Spacegray Base16 theme's embedded `author` field names Chris Kempson, and Spacegray's README links to his Base16 project. The Solarized README credits Ethan Schoonover and links to his original Solarized project. Both original projects' notices are preserved below in addition to the submodule redistribution notices. The original Base16 and Solarized references are supplemental upstream provenance snapshots; they are not additional syntect-pinned gitlinks.

All five notice sources below use MIT. Their notices are retained here with explicit mappings to the bundled syntect themes.

## Two Face grammar and theme notices

The upstream two-face 0.5.1 acknowledgement collection is reproduced below, including asset notices beyond the exposed language/theme subset because the grammar pack is bundled.

Most of the code for generating both theme and syntax dumps along with the
curation of said themes and syntaxes is taken from the
[`bat` project](https://github.com/sharkdp/bat).

## Addon native runtime dependency notices

The addon directly uses JNA 5.18.1 (with bundled libffi) on Android, and the host ExpoModulesCore / core editor. The host application supplies the full resolved Expo and React Native dependency graph; preserve those dependency notices for its actual selected versions.

### Java Native Access (JNA) 5.18.1 (Apache-2.0 option)
